v9.6.0 Release Notes\r\n## Detailed Changes\r\n### Alter step size and tick size via governance proposal\r\n* This release adds the ability for the community to modify the `StepBaseQuantums` and the `SubticksPerTick` configurations of a perpetual market via a governance proposal. These two are used as knobs to tune the step size and tick size respectively. 9.0 Release notes\r\n\r\n## Highlights\r\n\r\nThis release includes changes in the following major areas:\r\n\r\n- TWAP (Time weighted average price) Order support\r\n- Order router revenue sharing\r\n- Proposer Set Reduction\r\n\r\n## Bug Fixes\r\n\r\n- Ghost orders fix\r\n\r\n## Detailed Changes\r\n\r\n**TWAP**\r\n\r\n- This enables end users to submit orders that will be executed at certain time intervals at the current market price.\r\n\r\n**Order Router Rev Share**\r\n\r\n- Frontend order routers can now attribute their frontend to an order. This will allow them to receive a percentage of the maker or taker fee depending on the side of the order. The percentage will need to be voted in via governance.\r\n- Order router revenue share can be set via governance between 0 to 800,000 ppm.\r\n- If the user creating the order has a valid affiliate revenue share, the submitting order router will not receive a revenue split for either side of the fill. Affiliate revenue is capped at on a 30day rolling period.\r\n- Order router addresses are the wallets that funds will be sent to\r\n\r\n**Proposer Set Reduction**\r\n\r\n- Enables governance to select a subset of validators as proposers of blocks\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n* Revert \"Add Orderbook Mid Price Cache (#2289)\" by @adamfraser in https://github.com/dydxprotocol/v4-chain/pull/2333\r\n* 9.0 Release notes\r\n\r\n## Highlights\r\n\r\nThis release includes changes in the following major areas:\r\n\r\n- TWAP (Time weighted average price) Order support\r\n- Order router revenue sharing\r\n- Proposer Set Reduction\r\n\r\n## Bug Fixes\r\n\r\n- Ghost orders fix\r\n\r\n## Detailed Changes\r\n\r\n**TWAP**\r\n\r\n- This enables end users to submit orders that will be executed at certain time intervals at the current market price.\r\n\r\n**Order Router Rev Share**\r\n\r\n- Frontend order routers can now attribute their frontend to an order. This will allow them to receive a percentage of the maker or taker fee depending on the side of the order. The percentage will need to be voted in via governance.\r\n- Order router revenue share can be set via governance between 0 to 800,000 ppm.\r\n- If the user creating the order has a valid affiliate revenue share, the submitting order router will not receive a revenue split for either side of the fill. Affiliate revenue is capped at on a 30day rolling period.\r\n- Order router addresses are the wallets that funds will be sent to\r\n\r\n**Proposer Set Reduction**\r\n\r\n- Enables governance to select a subset of validators as proposers of blocks\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n\r\n* Revert \"Add Orderbook Mid Price Cache (#2289)\" by @adamfraser in Note: If using cosmosvisor, put binary under folder v8.1 which is the upgrade name\r\n\r\n## **Highlights**\r\n\r\nThis release includes changes in following major areas:\r\n\r\n- Builder codes\r\n- Clob transaction batching\r\n- Cross IML\r\n- New Market Liquidity Tier\r\n\r\n## **Detailed Changes**\r\n\r\n**Builder Codes**\r\n\r\n- This enables external parties to submit orders to dYdX and collect fees (per-order) for building and routing an order to the exchange.\r\n- The address and fee, in parts per million, needs to be configured via the BuilderCodeParameters in the order message itself. The fee will be paid out when the given order is filled.\r\n- Builder fees and addresses can be queried via the indexer using the /orders and /fills endpoints as usual. /orders contains details on the fee rate and builder address. /fills also contains the builder address as well as details on the amount charged per-fill.\r\n\r\n**Clob Transaction Batching**\r\n\r\n- This change allows multiple Clob messages (specifically pertaining to stateful orders) to be placed in a single transaction\r\n- This also allows for one transfer to be included in a batched transaction call, allowing users to transfer money into an account and place in trade with order enforced.\r\n\r\n**Cross Instant Market Listings**\r\n\r\n- Markets are now allowed to be cross-launchable from the outset. By tagging assets in the marketmap as cross-launcable, on launch it will be cross margin.\r\n\r\n**IML Liquidity Tier**\r\n\r\n- Markets launched will now use the new IML_5x liquidity tier\r\n\r\n##
